The shift disrupted the structured routines he had cultivated for years and added evening events and cross‑time‑zone meetings that encroached on his art activities, hiking, and mentoring at the community center. Over the next year, colleagues saw his reliability and polished demeanor give way to disorganized scheduling, missed follow‑through on projects, and increasingly unusual comments during meetings, while at home his spouse noticed abrupt changes in his energy, sleep, and engagement with family and community events. After Thomas showed up unannounced at the office on a weekend insisting he needed to \"document a hidden pattern\" in company communications and his supervisor found him confused and unable to account for recent mistakes, HR contacted his spouse, who brought him to a psychiatric evaluation when it was clear his functioning had seriously deteriorated.\n- Chief Complaint: “I’ve been seeing and hearing things at work and at home that no one else seems to notice, and it’s starting to scare me.”\n▸ Patient-specific Symptom Manifestations\n- S019 (Delusions): For months now I’ve been convinced that the emails and calendar invites going around the company are coded messages about me, like my new boss and people in other departments are quietly coordinating to test me or push me out, even when my wife and coworkers tell me it’s just normal office stuff.\n- S020 (Hallucinations): At the office and at home I keep hearing my name whispered from behind me and a man’s voice commenting on my work or warning me about “them,” and sometimes I see shadowy figures standing in the doorway or in the hallway that vanish when I look straight at them, and it’s gotten to the point where I can’t tell if I’m losing my mind or everyone else is missing something huge.\n- S023 (Negative_Symptoms): Outside of these high-energy spells, most days I just sit in my home office staring at the computer, feeling flat and blank, ignoring calls from friends and skipping the art group and hikes I used to love because I can’t seem to make myself care enough to get up and go.\n- S033 (Elevated_Irritable_Mood): I’ll go from feeling on top of the world and weirdly giddy—like I’m the only one who really understands how everything connects—to snapping at my wife and coworkers over tiny things, slamming doors or firing off sharp emails because I suddenly feel like everyone is against me or wasting my time.\n- S034 (Increased_Activity): When I’m in that revved-up mode, I’ll stay at my desk or pace the house all night starting spreadsheets, reorganizing files, sketching out big “communication systems” for the whole company and rewriting policies in my head, bouncing from task to task without actually finishing anything because I feel too wired to sit still.\n- S035 (Inflated_Self_Esteem): There are stretches where I honestly believe I’m the only person in the organization who can see the bigger pattern in all the communications, like I’ve been given this special ability to decode it and that senior leadership will eventually recognize me as the one who can fix everything if I just push hard enough.\n- S036 (Decreased_Sleep): Lately I can go two or three nights sleeping only a couple of hours, sometimes just dozing in my chair, and I wake up feeling charged and sure I don’t really need more sleep because my mind is racing with ideas I’m convinced I have to act on right away.\n- S037 (Pressured_Speech): Once I start talking about what I’m noticing—like the supposed patterns in emails or how meetings are connected—I can’t seem to stop; I jump from one thought to the next so fast that people cut me off or look confused, but I feel this pressure in my chest to keep explaining before the ideas disappear.\n▸ Additional Diagnostic Requirements\n- psychotic_symptoms_and_manic_episode_occur_concurrently: When I’m in those super-charged phases, barely sleeping and racing around with big plans for overhauling the company’s communication system, that’s also when the whispers get loudest and the shadowy figures seem to show up most, like the energy and the strange experiences are happening at the same time.\n- manic_episode_is_present_for_the_majority_of_the_total_duration_of_the_active_and_residual_portions_of_the_illness: Looking back over this past year or so, I feel like I’ve spent more time revved up—talking fast, sleeping very little, chasing big ideas about my special role at work—than in the flatter, drained periods in between.\n- delusions_and_hallucinations_occur_for_2_or_more_weeks_in_the_absence_of_a_major_mood_episode: There was a stretch earlier this year when my mood felt pretty neutral—no big highs or crashes—but I was still hearing my name whispered and was sure the emails were coded about me, even though my energy was low and I wasn’t buzzing with ideas like during my manic spells.\n- functional_impairment: Over the past several months I’ve missed deadlines, had projects taken away from me, stopped going to my art group and hikes, and my spouse has had to step in with HR and manage bills and appointments because I can’t keep my work or home responsibilities straight anymore.\n- duration: Ever since the reorganization about fifteen months ago, these swings in mood along with the strange experiences at work and home have been building and never really gone away.\n- psychotic_symptoms_duration: For roughly the past ten months, I’ve been dealing pretty steadily with the whispers, the shadowy figures, and the belief that company messages are secretly about me.\n- manic_episode_duration: For almost the whole past year, with only brief breaks, I’ve had these amped-up periods where I sleep very little, feel unusually important, and throw myself into grand plans for fixing the company’s systems.\n\nRules:\n- If the patient can specifically answer based on the profile, set matched=true, list for recent mistakes, HR contacted his spouse, who brought him to a psychiatric evaluation when it was clear his functioning had seriously deteriorated.\n- Chief Complaint: “I’ve been seeing and hearing things at work and at home that no one else seems to notice, and it’s starting to scare me.”\n▸ Patient-specific Symptom Manifestations\n- S019 (Delusions): For months now I’ve been convinced that the emails and calendar invites going around the company are coded messages about me, like my new boss and people in other departments are quietly coordinating to test me or push me out, even when my wife and coworkers tell me it’s just normal office stuff.\n- S020 (Hallucinations): At the office and at home I keep hearing my name whispered from behind me and a man’s voice commenting on my work or warning me about “them,” and sometimes I see shadowy figures standing in the doorway or in the hallway that vanish when I look straight at them, and it’s gotten to the point where I can’t tell if I’m losing my mind or everyone else is missing something huge.\n- S023 (Negative_Symptoms): Outside of these high-energy spells, most days I just sit in my home office staring at the computer, feeling flat and blank, ignoring calls from friends and skipping the art group and hikes I used to love because I can’t seem to make myself care enough to get up and go.\n- S033 (Elevated_Irritable_Mood): I’ll go from feeling on top of the world and weirdly giddy—like I’m the only one who really understands how everything connects—to snapping at my wife and coworkers over tiny things, slamming doors or firing off sharp emails because I suddenly feel like everyone is against me or wasting my time.\n- S034 (Increased_Activity): When I’m in that revved-up mode, I’ll stay at my desk or pace the house all night starting spreadsheets, reorganizing files, sketching out big “communication systems” for the whole company and rewriting policies in my head, bouncing from task to task without actually finishing anything because I feel too wired to sit still.\n- S035 (Inflated_Self_Esteem): There are stretches where I honestly believe I’m the only person in the organization who can see the bigger pattern in all the communications, like I’ve been given this special ability to decode it and that senior leadership will eventually recognize me as the one who can fix everything if I just push hard enough.\n- S036 (Decreased_Sleep): Lately I can go two or three nights sleeping only a couple of hours, sometimes just dozing in my chair, and I wake up feeling charged and sure I don’t really need more sleep because my mind is racing with ideas I’m convinced I have to act on right away.\n- S037 (Pressured_Speech): Once I start talking about what I’m noticing—like the supposed patterns in emails or how meetings are connected—I can’t seem to stop; I jump from one thought to the next so fast that people cut me off or look confused, but I feel this pressure in my chest to keep explaining before the ideas disappear.\n▸ Additional Diagnostic Requirements\n- psychotic_symptoms_and_manic_episode_occur_concurrently: When I’m in those super-charged phases, barely sleeping and racing around with big plans for overhauling the company’s communication system, that’s also when the whispers get loudest and the shadowy figures seem to show up most, like the energy and the strange experiences are happening at the same time.\n- manic_episode_is_present_for_the_majority_of_the_total_duration_of_the_active_and_residual_portions_of_the_illness: Looking back over It's honestly terrifying because it feels so constant and real.\n\nDoctor: Along with these experiences, have you been feeling persistently down, depressed, or lost interest in your usual activities during these past ten months?\n\nPatient: Yes, outside of my high-energy spells, I feel completely flat, blank, and drained. I've lost all interest in things I used to love, like my art group and hiking, and I just skip them because I can't bring myself to care enough to get up and go. It's like I have no motivation left on those days.\n\nDoctor: You mentioned having high-energy spells and low-energy periods. Do you ever experience the whispers or shadowy figures during times when your mood feels completely normal and stable?\n\nPatient: Yes, earlier this year there was a stretch of a few weeks where my mood felt completely neutral, with no big highs or crashes. Even though I wasn't buzzing with energy or big ideas, I was still hearing my name whispered and felt convinced the company emails were coded messages about me. It was scary because those experiences stayed even when my mood felt stable.\n\nDoctor: Looking back over the last ten months, would you say these high-energy spells and low-energy periods have been present for most of that time, or did they only happen occasionally?\n\nPatient: Yes, they've been present for the vast majority of the time. I've spent most of this past year in those revved-up, high-energy phases, barely sleeping and chasing big ideas with only brief breaks. Those amped-up periods have definitely dominated over the flatter, low-energy times.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy spells, how long do they typically last without stopping, and do you experience other symptoms like racing thoughts or speaking much faster than usual?\n\nPatient: These amped-up phases have been going on for almost the entire past year with only brief breaks, and my mind absolutely races with ideas I feel I have to act on right away. When I start talking about them, I speak so fast and jump from one thought to the next that people get confused or cut me off because of the intense pressure I feel to explain it all.\n\nDoctor: During these high-energy periods, have your racing thoughts and behaviors ever caused significant problems in your life, like major financial trouble, risky actions, or even needing to be hospitalized?"
  }
